# Every funder found, by type (issue #380, part 3)

1974 funders of 733 open-source-minded ventures, from [`data/investor_links.csv`](data/investor_links.csv). Each venture links to a page that names the funder in one of its rounds. **Bold** marks a venture where the funder led a round. U1–U4 are the venture groups in [`PROTOCOL.md`](PROTOCOL.md); a funder's type is set by hand in [`data/investor_types.csv`](data/investor_types.csv) (people are typed as angels). Counts are of ventures, not rounds. Read [`FUNDERS.md`](FUNDERS.md) for what the list shows and its limits.
